an observer that cannot distinguish execution times for noninterfering traces
(notice that
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{n_{1}}}}
in the definition of
\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}
is discarded). For
instance,

\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}(\{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}},n_{1})}}}\})=\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}(\{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}},n_{2})}}}\})
,
for any
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}
,
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{n_{1}}}}
,
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{n_{2}}}}
. Therefore, in this setting,
we know explicitly through
\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}

that a
\mathsf{CC^{\sim}}
compiler degrades source noninterference to target

timing-insensitive
noninterference.

Trace-Relating Compilation and ANI in General.

While the particular
\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}
and
\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}
above can be
discovered by intuition, we want to know whether there is a systematic
way of obtaining them in general.
In other words, for
any
trace relation
\sim
and
any
notion of
source NI, what property is guaranteed on noninterfering source programs by any

\mathsf{CC^{\sim}}
compiler?

We can now answer this question generally (
Theorem 4.1
): any source notion of
noninterference expressible as an instance of ANI is mapped to a corresponding
instance of ANI in the target, whenever source traces are an
abstraction of target ones (i.e., when
\sim
is a total and surjective map).
For this result we consider trace relations that can be split into input and
output trace relations (denoted as

\sim\;\triangleq\langle\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}$}\vss}}},\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}\rangle
) such that

\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}\sim\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}\iff\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}}}}\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}$}\vss}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}}}}\wedge\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}}\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}}
.
The trace relation
\sim
corresponds to a Galois connection between the
sets of trace properties

\tilde{\tau}\leftrightarrows\tilde{\sigma}
as described in

§ 2.2
. Similarly, the pair
\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}$}\vss}}}
and
\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}
corresponds to
a pair of Galois connections,

\tilde{\tau}^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}\leftrightarrows\tilde{\sigma}^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}
and

\tilde{\tau}^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}\leftrightarrows\tilde{\sigma}^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}
, between the sets
of input and output properties. In the timing example, time is an output so
we have
\sim\;\triangleq\langle=,\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}\rangle
and
\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}
is defined as

\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}}\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}}\equiv\exists\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{n}}}.~{}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}}=\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}},n)}}}
.

Theorem 4.1
(Compiling ANI)

Assume traces
of source and target languages are related via

\sim\;\subseteq{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{Trace_{S}}}}}\times{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{Trace_{T}}}}}
,

\sim\;\triangleq\langle\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}$}\vss}}},\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}\rangle
such that

\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}$}\vss}}}
and
\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}
are both total maps from target to source
traces, and
\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}$}\vss}}}
is surjective.
Assume
\downarrow
is a
\mathsf{CC^{\sim}}
compiler, and

\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\phi_{S}}}}\in\mathit{uco}(2^{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{Trace^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}_{S}}}}}),~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\rho_{S}}}}\in\mathit{uco}(2^{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{Trace^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}_{S}}}}})
.

If
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}
satisfies
\mathit{ANI}_{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\phi_{S}}}}}^{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\rho_{S}}}}}
, then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}

satisfies
\mathit{ANI}_{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}}^{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}}
, where

\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}
and
\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}
are defined as:

\displaystyle\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=g^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}\circ\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\phi_{S}}}}\circ f^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}};

\displaystyle\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=g^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}\circ\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\rho_{S}}}}\circ f^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}\quad\text{and}

\displaystyle f^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}(\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}_{\mathrm{T}}}}})

\displaystyle=\left\{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}}}}\,\mathrel{}\middle|\mathrel{}\,\exists\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}}}}\in\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}_{\mathrm{T}}}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}}}}\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}$}\vss}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}}}}\right\};

\displaystyle g^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\pi^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}_{S}}}})

\displaystyle=\left\{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}}}}\,\mathrel{}\middle|\mathrel{}\,\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}}}}\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}$}\vss}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}}}}\Rightarrow\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}}}}\in\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\pi^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}_{S}}}}\right\}

(and both
f^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}
and
g^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}
are defined analogously).

For the example above we recover the definitions we
justified intuitively, i.e.,
\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}=g^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}\circ\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\phi_{S}}}}\circ f^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}=\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}
and

\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}=g^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}\circ\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\rho_{S}}}}\circ f^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}=\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}
.
Moreover, we can prove that if
\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}
also is surjective,

\mathit{ANI}_{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}}^{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}}\subseteq\mathit{Cl}_{\subseteq}\circ\tilde{\tau}(\mathit{ANI}_{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\phi_{S}}}}}^{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\rho_{S}}}}})
.
Therefore, the derived guarantee

\mathit{ANI}_{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}}^{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}}
is at least as strong as
the one that follows by just knowing that the compiler
\downarrow
is

\mathsf{CC^{\sim}}
.

Noninterference and Undefined Behavior.
As stated above,

Theorem

4.1
does not apply to several scenarios from

§ 3
such as undefined behavior
(
§ 3.1
), as in those cases the relation

\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}
is not a total map. Nevertheless, we can still exploit our
framework to reason about the impact of compilation on
noninterference.

Let us consider
\sim\;\triangleq\langle\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}$}\vss}}},\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}\rangle

where
\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}$}\vss}}}
is any total and surjective map from target to source
inputs (e.g., equality) and
\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}
is defined as

\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}}\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}}\equiv\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}}=\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}}\vee\exists m^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}\leq\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}}={m^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}\cdot\mathit{\mathit{Goes\_wrong}}
.
Intuitively, a
\mathsf{CC^{\sim}}
compiler guarantees that no interference
can be observed by a target attacker that cannot exploit undefined
behavior to learn private information.
This intuition can be made formal by the following theorem.

Theorem 4.2
(Relaxed Compiling ANI)

Relax the assumptions of
Theorem

4.1
by allowing
\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}
to be

any
output trace relation.
If
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}
satisfies

\mathit{ANI}_{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\phi_{S}}}}}^{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\rho_{S}}}}}
, then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}
satisfies

\mathit{ANI}_{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}}^{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}}
where

\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}
is defined as in
Theorem

4.1
, and

\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}
is such that:

\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}~{}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}}\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}}\Rightarrow~{}\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}(\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}}}}})=\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}(\tilde{\tau}^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\rho_{S}}}}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}})}}})).

Technically, instead of giving us a
definition
of
\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}
, the
theorem gives a
property
of it. The property states that, given a target
output trace
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}}}}
, the attacker cannot distinguish it from any other
target output traces produced by other possible compilations
(
\tilde{\tau}^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}
) of the source trace
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}
it relates to, up to the
observational power of the source level attacker
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\rho_{S}}}}
.
Therefore, given a source attacker
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\rho_{S}}}}
,
the theorem characterizes a
family
of attackers that cannot
observe any interference for a correctly compiled noninterfering
program.
Notice that the target attacker

\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}=\lambda\_.~{}\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\top}}}
satisfies the premise
of the theorem, but defines a trivial hyperproperty, so that we cannot
prove in general that

\mathit{ANI}_{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}}^{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}}\subseteq\mathit{Cl}_{\subseteq}\circ\tilde{\tau}(\mathit{ANI}_{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\phi_{S}}}}}^{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\rho_{S}}}}})
.
The same
\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}}}=\lambda\_.~{}\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\top}}}
shows
that the family of attackers described in
Theorem

4.2
is
nonempty, and this ensures the existence of a most powerful
attacker among them
[
21
]
, whose explicit characterization we
leave for future work.

From Target NI to Source NI.

We now explore the dual question: under what hypotheses does trace-relating compiler
correctness alone allow
target noninterference to be reduced to source noninterference?
This is of practical interest, as one would be able to
protect from target attackers by ensuring noninterference in the
source. This task can be made easier if the source language has some
static enforcement mechanism
[
1
,
37
]
.

Let us consider the languages from
§ 3.4
extended
with inputting of (pairs of) values.
It is easy to show that the compiler described in
§ 3.4
is still
\mathsf{CC^{\sim}}
.
Assume that we want to satisfy a given notion of target noninterference after
compilation, i.e.,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\models}}}}\mathit{ANI}_{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}^{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}
.
Recall that the observational power of the target attacker,

\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}
, is expressed as a property of sequences of values. To
express the same property (or attacker) in the source, we have to
abstract the way pairs of values are nested. For instance, the source
attacker should not distinguish

\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\langle v_{1},\langle v_{2},v_{3}\rangle\rangle}}}
and

\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\langle\langle v_{1},v_{2}\rangle,v_{3}\rangle}}}
.
In general (i.e., when
\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}$}\vss}}}
is not the identity), this argument is
valid only when
\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}
can be represented in the source. More
precisely,
\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}
must consider as equivalent all target inputs
that are related to the same source one, because in the source it
is not possible to have a finer distinction of inputs.
This intuitive correspondence can be formalized as follows:

Theorem 4.3
(Target ANI by source ANI)

Let

\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}\in\mathit{uco}(2^{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{Trace^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}_{T}}}}})
,

\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}\in\mathit{uco}(2^{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{Trace^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}_{T}}}}})
and
\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}$}\vss}}}
a total and
surjective map from source outputs to target ones and assume that

\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}~{}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}}}}\mathrel{\mathop{\sim}\limits^{\vbox to-0.5pt{\kern-2.0pt\hbox{\tiny$\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}$}\vss}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}}}}\Rightarrow\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}(\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}}}})=\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}(\tilde{\tau}^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}}}})).

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\cdot}}}\right\downarrow}}
is a
\mathsf{CC^{\sim}}
compiler and
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}

satisfies
\mathit{ANI}_{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\phi^{\#}_{S}}}}}^{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{S}}}}}
, then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}

satisfies
\mathit{ANI}_{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}^{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}}
for

\displaystyle\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\phi^{\#}_{S}}}}=

\displaystyle\ \tilde{\sigma}^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}}\circ\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\phi}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}\circ\tilde{\tau}^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\circ}};

\displaystyle\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\rho^{\#}_{S}}}}=

\displaystyle\ \tilde{\sigma}^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}\circ\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rho}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{T}}}}\circ\tilde{\tau}^{\scaleobj{0.7}{\bullet}}.

To wrap up the discussion about noninterference, the results presented in this section formalize and generalize some intuitive facts about compiler correctness and noninterference. Of course, they all place some restrictions on the shape of the noninterference instances that can
be considered, because compiler correctness alone is in general not a strong enough criterion for dealing with many security properties
[
6
,
17
]
.

5
Trace-Relating Secure Compilation

So far we have studied compiler correctness criteria
for whole, standalone programs.
However, in practice, programs do not exist in isolation, but in a
context where they interact with other programs, libraries, etc. In many cases, this context cannot be assumed to be benign and
could instead behave maliciously to try to disrupt a compiled program.

Hence, in this section we consider the following
secure compilation

scenario: a source program is compiled and linked with an arbitrary
target-level context, i.e., one that may not be expressible as the compilation of
a source context.
Compiler correctness does not address this case, as it does not consider
arbitrary target contexts,
looking instead at whole programs (empty context
[
34
]
)
or
well-behaved target contexts that behave like source ones (as in
compositional compiler
correctness
[
46
,
63
,
27
,
31
]
).

To account for this scenario,
Abate et al.
[
2
]
describe several secure
compilation criteria based on the preservation of classes of (hyper)properties (e.g., trace properties, safety, hypersafety, hyperproperties, etc.) against
arbitrary target contexts.
For each of these criteria, they give an equivalent “property-free” criterion,
analogous to the equivalence between
\mathsf{TP}
and
\mathsf{CC}^{=}
.
For instance, their
robust
trace property preservation criterion (
\mathsf{RTP}
)
states that, for any trace property
\pi
, if a source
partial
program
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}
plugged into any
context
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{C_{S}}}}
satisfies
\pi
, then the compiled
program
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}\right\downarrow}}
plugged into any target context
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{C_{T}}}}
satisfies

\pi
.
Their equivalent criterion to
\mathsf{RTP}
is
\mathsf{RTC}
, which states that for any trace
produced by the compiled program, when linked with any target context, there is a
source context that produces the same trace. Formally (writing
C\left[P\right]
to
mean the whole program that results from linking partial program
P
with
context
C
) they define:

\displaystyle\mathsf{RTP}\equiv

\displaystyle\ \forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}.~{}\forall\pi.~{}(\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{C_{S}}}}.~{}\forall t.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{C_{S}\left[P\right]}}}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\bm{\rightsquigarrow}}}}t\Rightarrow t\in\pi)\Rightarrow(\forall\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{C_{T}}}}.~{}\forall\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}.~{}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{C_{T}\left[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}\right\downarrow}}\right]}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\bm{\rightsquigarrow}}}}t\Rightarrow t\in\pi);

\displaystyle\mathsf{RTC}\equiv

\displaystyle\ \forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}.~{}\forall\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{C_{T}}}}.\forall t.\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{C_{T}\left[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}\right\downarrow}}\right]}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\bm{\rightsquigarrow}}}}t\Rightarrow\exists\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{C_{S}}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{C_{S}\left[P\right]}}}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\bm{\rightsquigarrow}}}}t.

In the following we adopt the notation
P\models_{R}\pi
to mean
“
P

robustly
satisfies
\pi
,”
i.e.,
P
satisfies
\pi
irrespective of the contexts it is linked with.
Thus, we write more compactly:

\displaystyle\mathsf{RTP}\equiv\forall\pi.~{}\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\models_{R}}}}\pi\Rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}\right\downarrow}}~{}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\models}}}_{R}}}}\pi.

All the criteria of
Abate et al.
[
2
]

share this flavor of stating the existence of some source context that
simulates the behavior of any given target context, with some variations
depending on the class of (hyper)properties under consideration.
All these criteria are
stated in a setting where source and target traces are the same.
In this section, we extend their result to our trace-relating
setting, obtaining
trintarian views for secure compilation. Despite the similarities with

§ 2
, more challenges show up, in
particular when considering the robust preservation of proper
sub-classes of trace properties. For example, after
application of
\tilde{\sigma}
or
\tilde{\tau}
, a property may not be
safety anymore, a crucial point for the
equivalence with the property-free criterion for safety properties by

Abate et al.
[
2
]
.
We solve this by interpreting the class of safety
properties as an
abstraction
of the class of all trace
properties induced by a closure operator (
§ 5.1
).
The remaining subsections provide example compilation chains satisfying our
trace-relating secure compilation criteria for trace properties
(
§ 5.2
) and for safety properties
hypersafety (
§ 5.3
).

5.1
Trace-Relating Secure Compilation: A Spectrum of Trinities

In this subsection we generalize many of the criteria of
Abate et al.
[
2
]
using
the ideas of
§ 2
.
Before discussing how we solve the challenges for classes such as
safety and hypersafety, we show the simple generalization of

\mathsf{RTC}
to the trace-relating setting (
\mathsf{RTC^{\sim}}
) and its corresponding
trinitarian view (
Theorem

5.1
):

Theorem 5.1
(Trinity for Robust Trace Properties

)

For any trace relation
\sim
and induced property mappings

\tilde{\tau}
and
\tilde{\sigma}
, we have:
\mathsf{RTP^{\tilde{\tau}}}\iff\mathsf{RTC^{\sim}}\iff\mathsf{RTP^{\tilde{\sigma}}}
, where

\displaystyle\mathsf{RTC^{\sim}}

\displaystyle\equiv\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}~{}\forall\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{C_{T}}}}~{}\forall\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}.~{}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{C_{T}\left[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}\right\downarrow}}\right]}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\bm{\rightsquigarrow}}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}\Rightarrow\exists\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{C_{S}}}}~{}\exists\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}\sim\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{C_{S}\left[P\right]}}}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\bm{\rightsquigarrow}}}}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}};

\displaystyle\mathsf{RTP^{\tilde{\tau}}}

\displaystyle\equiv\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}~{}\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\pi_{S}}}}\in 2^{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{{Trace}_{S}}}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\models_{R}}}}~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\pi_{S}}}}\Rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}\right\downarrow}}~{}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\models}}}_{R}}}}~{}\tilde{\tau}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\pi_{S}}}});

\displaystyle\mathsf{RTP^{\tilde{\sigma}}}

\displaystyle\equiv\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}~{}\forall\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}}\in 2^{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{{Trace}_{T}}}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\models_{R}}}}~{}\tilde{\sigma}(\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}})\Rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}\right\downarrow}}~{}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\models}}}_{R}}}}~{}\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}}.

Abate et al.
[
2
]
propose many more equivalent pairs of criteria,
each preserving different classes of (hyper)properties, which we
briefly recap now. For trace properties, they also have criteria
that preserve
safety properties plus their version of liveness properties. For
hyperproperties, they have criteria that preserve hypersafety
properties, subset-closed hyperproperties, and arbitrary
hyperproperties. Finally, they define
relational

hyperproperties, which are relations between the behaviors
of multiple programs for
expressing, e.g., that a program always runs faster than another.
For relational hyperproperties, they have criteria that preserve arbitrary
relational properties, relational safety properties, relational hyperproperties
and relational subset-closed hyperproperties. Roughly speaking, the
security guarantees due to robust preservation of trace properties
regard only protecting the integrity of the program from the context,
the guarantees of hyperproperties also regard data confidentiality,
and the guarantees of relational hyperproperties
even regard code confidentiality.
Naturally, these stronger guarantees are increasingly harder to enforce and prove.

While we have lifted the most significant criteria from
Abate et al.
[
2
]

to our trinitarian view, due to space constraints we provide the
formal definitions only for the two most interesting criteria.
We summarize the generalizations of many other criteria in

Figure 2
, described at the end.
Omitted definitions are available in the online appendix.

Beyond Trace Properties: Robust Safety and Hyperproperty Preservation.

We detail robust preservation of safety properties and of arbitrary
hyperproperties since they are both relevant from a security point of
view and their generalization is interesting.

Theorem 5.2
(Trinity for Robust Safety Properties

)

For any trace relation
\sim
and for the induced property mappings
\tilde{\tau}
and
\tilde{\sigma}
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathsf{RTP^{\mathit{Safe}\circ\tilde{\tau}}}\iff\mathsf{RSC^{\sim}}\iff\mathsf{RSP^{\tilde{\sigma}}},

where

\displaystyle\mathsf{RSC^{\sim}}

\displaystyle\equiv\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}~{}\forall\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{C_{T}}}}~{}\forall\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}~{}\forall\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{m}}}\leq\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}.\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{C_{T}\left[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}\right\downarrow}}\right]}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\bm{\rightsquigarrow}}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}\Rightarrow\exists\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{C_{S}}}}~{}\exists\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\prime}}}}\geq\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{m}}}~{}\exists\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}\sim\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t^{\prime}}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{C_{S}\left[P\right]}}}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\bm{\rightsquigarrow}}}}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}};

\displaystyle\mathsf{RTP^{\mathit{Safe}\circ\tilde{\tau}}}

\displaystyle\equiv\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\pi_{S}}}}\in 2^{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{{Trace}_{S}}}}}.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\models_{R}}}}~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\pi_{S}}}}\Rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}\right\downarrow}}~{}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\models}}}_{R}}}}~{}(\mathit{Safe}\circ\tilde{\tau})(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\pi_{S}}}});

\displaystyle\mathsf{RSP^{\tilde{\sigma}}}

\displaystyle\equiv\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}\forall\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}}\in\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{Safety_{T}}}}.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\models_{R}}}}~{}\tilde{\sigma}(\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}})\Rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{P}}}\right\downarrow}}~{}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\models}}}_{R}}}}~{}\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}}.

There is an interesting asymmetry between the last two characterizations above,
which we explain now in more detail.

\mathsf{RSP^{\tilde{\sigma}}}
quantifies over target safety properties, while

\mathsf{RTP^{\mathit{Safe}\circ\tilde{\tau}}}
quantifies over
arbitrary
source properties,
but imposes the composition of
\tilde{\tau}
with
\mathit{Safe}
, which maps
an arbitrary target property
\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}}
to the target safety property
that best over-approximates
\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}}
8
8
8
\mathit{Safe}(\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}})=\cap\left\{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{S_{\mathrm{T}}}}}\,\mathrel{}\middle|\mathrel{}\,\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}}\subseteq\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{S_{\mathrm{T}}}}}\wedge\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{S_{\mathrm{T}}}}}\in\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{Safety_{T}}}}\right\}

,
