Appendix 0.B
Preserving Other (Hyper)Property Classes

In this section we investigate how to preserve two classes of (hyper)properties
beyond trace properties: safety properties (
§ 0.B.1
)
and arbitrary hyperproperties that are not just subset-closed
(
§ 0.B.2
).
For each of these classes, we start by giving an intuition of what it means to
preserve such a class in the equal-trace setting, then we study preservation of
that class in the trace-relating setting.
In both cases, we obtain a somewhat weaker preservation result that
reflects the loss of precision incurred by the properties.

0.B.1
Preserving Safety Properties

Safety
collects all trace properties prescribing that
“something
bad never happens,”
so that their violation can be monitored and, when
observed, no longer restored
[
14
]
.
In order to define this class, we assume traces are built in a standard manner
as lists or streams of events, so that we can consider
finite prefixes

m
of a trace
t
, written
m\leq t
.

Definition 8
(Safety Properties)

A property
\pi
is a
safety property
if

\forall t\notin\pi.~{}\exists m\leq t.~{}\forall t^{\prime}.~{}m\leq t^{\prime}\Rightarrow t^{\prime}\notin\pi
.

A proof of the preservation of all trace properties, i.e., of
\mathsf{CC^{=}}
,
may require to show that a source program can produce an infinite stream of events.
In order to prove the same for safety properties, one only has to
show the following weaker variant of
\mathsf{CC^{=}}
:

\mathsf{SC^{=}}\equiv\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}},m\ldotp{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\bm{\rightsquigarrow}}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\mathit{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{m}}}}}}\Rightarrow\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\bm{\rightsquigarrow}}}}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\mathit{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{m}}}}}}

where
W{\bm{\rightsquigarrow}}m
means that
W
produces the finite prefix
m
, i.e., that
m

is a prefix of a trace produced by
W
, that is
\exists t\ldotp m\leq t\wedge W{\bm{\rightsquigarrow}}t
.

Recalling the intention of safety properties to prescribe
“something bad
never happens,”

W{\bm{\rightsquigarrow}}m
can be interpreted as a violation of a
safety property, with
m
being a bad prefix.
The statement of
\mathsf{SC^{=}}
can therefore be read as
“whenever
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}

violates a safety property, then
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}
does.”

By contraposition, this was proven equivalent to the preservation of arbitrary
safety properties
[
2
]
:

\mathsf{SC^{=}}\iff\forall\pi\in\mathit{Safety}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\models}}}~{}\pi\Rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}~{}\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\models}}}~{}\pi

In the trace-relating setting, we generalize
\mathsf{SC^{=}}
by requiring that if
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}
violates
a target safety property
\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}}\in\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{Safety_{T}}}}
, then
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}
violates its source interpretation,
\tilde{\sigma}(\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}})
.

Definition 9
(
\mathsf{SC^{\sim}}
)

Given a trace relation
\sim\subseteq\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{Trace_{S}}}}\times\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{Trace_{T}}}}
, a compilation chain
\downarrow
is
\mathsf{SC^{\sim}}
if and only if the following holds:

\displaystyle\mathsf{SC^{\sim}}\equiv\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\ldotp\forall

\displaystyle\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{m}}}\ldotp{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\bm{\rightsquigarrow}}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{m}}}\Rightarrow\exists\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}\ldotp\exists\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}\ldotp\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{m}}}\leq\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}\wedge\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}\sim\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}\wedge\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\bm{\rightsquigarrow}}}}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}

Again, we propose two generalizations of the preservation of safety properties
that are equivalent to
\mathsf{SC^{\sim}}
.
Similarly to what we had to do in
§ 2.3
, we have to take
into account the fact that for
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\pi_{S}}}}\in\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{Safety_{S}}}}
,

\tilde{\tau}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\pi_{S}}}})
is not necessarily in
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{Safety_{T}}}}
.
Hence, we have to close
\tilde{\tau}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\pi_{S}}}})
in the class of target safety
properties.

Theorem 0.B.1
(Trinitarian view for Safety)

For a trace relation
\sim\ \subseteq\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{Trace_{S}}}}\times\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{Trace_{T}}}}
and its induced property mappings
\tilde{\sigma}
and
\tilde{\tau}
,

\mathsf{SC^{\sim}}
is equivalent to these criteria:

\displaystyle\mathsf{SP^{\tilde{\sigma}}}

\displaystyle\equiv\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}~{}\forall\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}}\in\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{Safety_{T}}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\models}}}~{}\tilde{\sigma}(\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}})\Rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}~{}\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\models}}}~{}\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{TP^{\mathit{Safe}\circ\tilde{\tau}}}

\displaystyle\equiv\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}~{}\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\pi_{S}}}}\in 2^{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{{Trace}_{S}}}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\models}}}~{}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\pi_{S}}}})\Rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}~{}\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\models}}}~{}(\mathit{Safe}\circ\tilde{\tau})(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\pi_{S}}}})

\sim

\mathsf{SC^{\sim}}
\mathsf{SP^{\tilde{\sigma}}}
\mathsf{TP^{\mathit{Safe}\circ\tilde{\tau}}}

where
\mathit{Safe}(\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}})=\bigcap\left\{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{S_{T}}}}\,\mathrel{}\middle|\mathrel{}\,\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{S_{T}}}}\in\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{Safety_{T}}}}\wedge\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}}\subseteq\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{S_{T}}}}\right\}
is the smallest target safety property that
contains
\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\pi_{\mathrm{T}}}}}
.

Notice that in
\mathsf{TP^{\mathit{Safe}\circ\tilde{\tau}}}
, we quantify over arbitrary source properties,
but the guarantee we get in the target is given by approximating the guarantee
defined by
\tilde{\tau}
, with a safety property.
This second approximation is optimal by minimality of
\mathrm{Safe}
, and is
necessary as
\tilde{\tau}
may not map safety properties to safety
properties (in this case, no comparison with
\mathsf{SC^{\sim}}
would be
possible).

0.B.2
Preserving Non-Subset Closed Hyperproperties

Many hyperproperties of interest, including possibilistic
information-flow policy are not subset-closed
[
14
]
.
\mathsf{CC^{=}}
is not enough to
guarantee the preservation of such hyperproperties, as it only ensures
refinement of the behaviors through compilation but not the other
inclusion, sometimes referred to as
reflection
.

It is easy to show that that the following strengthening of
\mathsf{CC^{=}}
is equivalent to the preservation of arbitrary hyperproperties.

Theorem 0.B.2
(
\mathsf{HC^{=}}
,
\mathsf{HP}
)

For every compilation chain
\downarrow
, the followings are equivalent

\displaystyle\mathsf{HC^{=}}

\displaystyle\equiv~{}\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}.~{}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{beh({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}})}}}=\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{beh(W)}}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{HP}

\displaystyle\equiv~{}\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\forall H\in 2^{2^{\text{Trace}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\models}}}~{}H\Rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}~{}\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\models}}}~{}H

The generalization to the trace-relating setting,

\mathsf{HC^{\sim}}\equiv~{}\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}.~{}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{beh({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}})}}}=\tilde{\tau}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{beh(W)}}})
,
does not come with two equivalent formulations in terms of
preservation of hyperproperties.
While for
\mathsf{HP^{\tilde{\tau}}}
the equivalence holds unconditionally, for

\mathsf{HP^{\tilde{\sigma}}}
some extra assumptions are required, depending on the
implication one is interested in. It is still possible, and correct, to deduce a
source obligation for a given target hyperproperty
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{H_{T}}}}
when no information
is lost in the the composition
\tilde{\tau}\circ\tilde{\sigma}
(i.e., the two
maps are a Galois
insertion
).

Vice versa
,
\mathsf{HC^{\sim}}
is a consequence of
\mathsf{HP^{\tilde{\sigma}}}
when no information is
lost in composing in the other direction,
\tilde{\sigma}\circ\tilde{\tau}

(i.e., the two maps are a Galois
reflection
).

Theorem 0.B.3
(Weak Trinity)

Given a trace relation
\sim\subseteq\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{Trace_{S}}}}\times\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{Trace_{T}}}}
, and
its induced property mappings
\tilde{\sigma}
and
\tilde{\tau}
,
\mathsf{HC^{\sim}}

is equivalent to
\mathsf{HP^{\tilde{\tau}}}
below. Moreover if

\tilde{\tau}\leftrightarrows\tilde{\sigma}
is a Galois insertion (i.e.,
\tilde{\tau}\circ\tilde{\sigma}=id
),
\mathsf{HC^{\sim}}
implies
\mathsf{HP^{\tilde{\sigma}}}
,
while if
\tilde{\sigma}\leftrightarrows\tilde{\tau}
is a Galois reflection
(i.e.,
\tilde{\sigma}\circ\tilde{\tau}=id
),
\mathsf{HP^{\tilde{\sigma}}}
implies

\mathsf{HC^{\sim}}
.

\displaystyle\mathsf{HP^{\tilde{\tau}}}

\displaystyle\equiv\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}~{}\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{H_{S}}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\models}}}~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{H_{S}}}}\Rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}~{}\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\models}}}~{}\tilde{\tau}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{H_{S}}}})

\displaystyle\mathsf{HP^{\tilde{\sigma}}}

\displaystyle\equiv\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}~{}\forall\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{H_{T}}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\models}}}~{}\tilde{\sigma}(\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{H_{T}}}})\Rightarrow{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}~{}\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\models}}}~{}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{H_{T}}}}

Appendix 0.C
Relation to Melton et al. and Sabry and Wadler

In this section we assume that source and target languages are
equipped with operational small steps semantics,
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\to}}}
and

\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\to}}}
respectively. Let
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{State_{S}}}}
and
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{State_{T}}}}
be the
set of all possible program states in the source and in the target
respectively. We define traces as finite or infinite sequences of
program states, formally

\displaystyle\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{Trace_{S}}}}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{State_{S}}}}^{*}\cup\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{State_{S}}}}^{\omega}

\displaystyle\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{Trace_{T}}}}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{State_{T}}}}^{*}\cup\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{State_{T}}}}^{\omega}.

For both source and target languages, we say that a program produces
a trace if and only if it can be extracted by an admissible execution
of the program itself. Formally

\displaystyle\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W\rightsquigarrow}}}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}\iff

\displaystyle\exists(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W_{0}\to W_{1}\to\ldots\to~{}W_{k}\to\ldots}}}).

\displaystyle~{}\exists~{}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{0<i_{1}<i_{2}<\ldots<i_{k}<\ldots}}}).

\displaystyle~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W_{0}}}}=\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s_{o}}}}\wedge\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{j}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s_{j}}}}=\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W_{i_{j}}}}}.

a similar definition is given for the target, based on the target
operational semantics
\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\to}}}
. The compilation induces a
relation between source and target states,

\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s_{k}}}}\sim\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t_{h}}}}\iff{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s_{k}}}}\right\downarrow}}=\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t_{h}}}}

that we lift to traces by requiring the traces to be pointwise related,

\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}\sim\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}\iff\forall n.~{}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s_{n}}}}\right\downarrow}}=\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t_{n}}}}

Recall the definition of
\mathsf{CC^{\sim}}

\mathsf{CC^{\sim}}\equiv\forall\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}.~{}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}{\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\rightsquigarrow}}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}\Rightarrow\exists\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}\sim\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}.~{}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W\rightsquigarrow}}}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}

that spelled out states that anytime we extract some states from the
execution of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}
, it is possible to extract the same number of
source, and related, states from some execution of
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}
. In
other words
\mathsf{CC^{\sim}}
requires that whenever
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}
reduces in an
arbitrary (but finite) amount of steps to some target state,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\right\downarrow}}\bm{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\to^{*}}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t_{k}}}}
, then
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{W}}}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\to^{*}}}}\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s_{h}}}}

for some state
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s_{h}}}}\sim\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t_{k}}}}
, i.e.,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s_{h}}}}\right\downarrow}}=\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t_{k}}}}
.

This captures is in general a weaker requirement than the existence of the
decompilation function in
Melton et al.
[
39
]
and

Sabry and Wadler
[
59
]
, but coincide for the case study in

Melton et al.
[
39
]
, where
\downarrow
is an injective map.

0.D.1
Additional Formalism for
Section

3.3

The Source Language

Below is the syntax of the source language.

\displaystyle\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e}}}\mathrel{::=}

\displaystyle\ \mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{n}}}\mid\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{b}}}\mid\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e\ \mathord{op}\ e}}}\mid\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{{if}~{}e~{}{then}~{}e~{}{else}~{}e}}}\mid\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{in_{b}}}}\mid\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{in_{n}}}}\qquad\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{op}}}\mathrel{::=}\ \mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{+}}}\mid\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\times}}}\mid\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\leq}}}\qquad\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{ty}}}\mathrel{::=}\ \displaystyle\ \mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{n}}}\mid\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{b}}}\mid\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{error}}}\qquad\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{i}}}\mathrel{::=}\ \mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{n}}}\mid\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{b}}}\qquad\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{is}}}\mathrel{::=}\ \mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{i\cdot is}}}\mid\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\varnothing}}}\qquad\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}\mathrel{::=}\ \mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left\langle is,r\right\rangle}}}

Well-typed programs do not produce
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{error}}}
(

, see Theorem type_soundness in file
TypeRelationExampleInput.v
).

Below is the big-step semantics of the source language.

\begin{array}[]{c}\textsf{\scriptsize({Sem-nat})}\\

\begin{array}[]{c}\textsf{\scriptsize({Sem-in-nat})}\\

\begin{array}[]{c}\textsf{\scriptsize({Sem-op-nat})}\\
\inference{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e_{1}\bm{\rightsquigarrow}\left\langle is_{1},n_{1}\right\rangle}}}&\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e_{2}\bm{\rightsquigarrow}\left\langle is_{2},n_{2}\right\rangle}}}&\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{op}}}\in\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\{+,\times\}}}}}{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e_{1}\ \mathord{op}\ e_{2}\bm{\rightsquigarrow}\left\langle is_{1}\cdot is_{2},(n_{1}\ \mathord{op}\ n_{2})\right\rangle}}}}\end{array}

The Target Language

Below is the syntax of the target language.

3.4

The Source Language
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{N^{p}}}}

\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{N^{p}}}}
is a statically-typed language with expressions, commands and simple types (natural numbers and pairs) whose syntax is presented below.
The key aspect of
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{N^{p}}}}
is the primitive for sending
pairs
over a network interface.
The type system of
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{N^{p}}}}
is unsurprising.

\displaystyle\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{c}}}\mathrel{::=}

\displaystyle\ The Target Language
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{N^{n}}}}

\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{N^{n}}}}
is a statically-typed language with the same types as
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{N^{p}}}}
, but its primitive for sending over a network interface only sends
natural numbers
.
Nothing changes regarding statements, terms, values, types and evaluation contexts between
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{N^{n}}}}
and
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{N^{p}}}}
.
There is only one typing rule that changes compared to
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{N^{p}}}}
, the one for
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{send~{}n}}}
.

\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{N^{n}}}}
has the same dynamic semantics as
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{N^{p}}}}
, the only changes regard the nature of messages and the reduction of
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{send~{}n}}}
.

\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{M}}}\mathrel{::=}\ \mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{n}}}

\begin{array}[]{c}\textsf{\scriptsize({Type-$\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{N^{n}}}}$-send})}\\
\inference{\vdash\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{e}}}:\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{N}}}}{\vdash\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{send~{}e}}}}}}}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{c}\textsf{\scriptsize({Eval-$\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{N^{n}}}}$-Send})}\\
\inference{}{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{send~{}n}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{~{}\mathrel{\xrightarrow{~{}n~{}}}~{}}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{skip}}}}}}}\end{array}

The Compiler from
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{N^{p}}}}
to
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{N^{n}}}}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left(\cdot\right)}}}\right\downarrow}}

The compiler from
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{N^{p}}}}
to
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{N^{n}}}}
is defined inductively on the type derivation of a source statement (
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\cdot}}}\right\downarrow}}:\ \vdash\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{c}}}\to\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{c}}}
) which, in turn, relies on compilation of expressions (
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\cdot}}}\right\downarrow}}:\ \vdash\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e:\tau}}}\to\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{e}}}
).
The only interesting case is when compiling a
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{send~{}e}}}}}}
, where we use the source type information concerning the message (i.e., a pair) being sent to deconstruct that pair into a sequence of natural numbers, which is what is sent in the target.

The compiler operates on
type derivations
for terms.
Thus, compiling
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{c;c^{\prime}}}}
would look like the following (using
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{D}}}
as a metavariable to range over derivations).

However, note that each judgment uniquely identifies which typing rule is being applied and the underlying derivation.
Thus, for compactness, we only write the judgment in the compilation and implicitly apply the related typing rule to obtain the underlying judgments for recursive calls.

\displaystyle\ =\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\mathtt{gensend}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left(\vdash\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e:\tau\times\tau^{\prime}}}}\right)}}}\right\downarrow}}\right)}}}}}}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left(\vdash\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{c;c^{\prime}}}}\right)}}}\right\downarrow}}

\displaystyle\ =\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left(\vdash\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{c}}}\right)}}}\right\downarrow}};{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left(\vdash\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{c^{\prime}}}}\right)}}}\right\downarrow}}}}}

The
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\mathtt{gensend}\left(\cdot\right)}}}
function takes a source expression and returns a sequence of instructions sending each element of the expression.
Formally:
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\mathtt{gensend}\left(\cdot\right)}}}:\ \vdash\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e:\tau}}}\to\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{c}}}

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\mathtt{gensend}\left(\vdash\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e:\tau}}}\right)}}}=\begin{cases}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{send~{}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left(\vdash\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{{e}}}}:N\right)}}}\right\downarrow}}}}}}}}}&\text{ if }\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\tau}}}=\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{N}}}\\
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\mathtt{gensend}\left(\vdash\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e.1:\tau^{\prime}}}}\right)}}};{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\mathtt{gensend}\left(\vdash\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e.2:\tau^{\prime\prime}}}}\right)}}}}}}&\text{ if }\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\tau}}}=\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\tau^{\prime}\times\tau^{\prime\prime}}}}\end{cases}

0.D.3
Additional Lemmas and Proofs of
Section

3.4

To prove that the compiler is correct (
Theorem

3.2
) we need two auxiliary results.

Lemma

4
tells us that the way we break down a source send into multiple target ones is correct.

Lemma

5
tells us that the compilation of expressions is correct.

Lemma 4
(
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\mathtt{gensend}\left(\cdot\right)}}}
works)

\text{ if }\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\mathtt{gensend}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left(\vdash\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e:\tau\times\tau^{\prime}}}}\right)}}}\right\downarrow}}\right)}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{~{}\mathrel{\xrightarrow{~{}t~{}}}~{}}}}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{skip}}}}}}\text{ then }\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e\ \hookrightarrow\ \!\!\!^{*}\ v}}}\text{ and }\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{v}}}\operatorname{\sim}\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}

Proof

We proceed by induction on
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\tau}}}
and
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\tau^{\prime}}}}
:

\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\tau}}}=\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{N}}}
and
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\tau^{\prime}}}}=\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{N}}}

By canonicity we have that
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e}}}=\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left\langle n,n^{\prime}\right\rangle}}}

{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\mathtt{gensend}\left(\cdot\right)}}}
translates that into
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{send~{}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left(\vdash{\left\langle n,n^{\prime}\right\rangle.1:N}\right)}}}\right\downarrow}}}}};\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{send~{}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left(\vdash\left\langle n,n^{\prime}\right\rangle.2:N\right)}}}\right\downarrow}}}}}}}}
.

By the target semantics and
Section

0.D.2
, that produces
\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}=\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{n;n^{\prime}}}}
.

We need to prove that

•

\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e\equiv\left\langle n,n^{\prime}\right\rangle\ \hookrightarrow\ \!\!\!^{*}\ \left\langle n,n;\right\rangle}}}
which is trivially true;

•

\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left\langle n,n^{\prime}\right\rangle}}}\sim\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{n;n^{\prime}}}}
, which holds by
Section

3.4
.

\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\tau}}}=\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\tau_{1}\times\tau_{1}^{\prime}}}}
and
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\tau^{\prime}}}}=\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\tau_{2}\times\tau_{2}^{\prime}}}}

So by canonicity
\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{e}}}=\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left\langle\left\langle e_{1},e_{1}^{\prime}\right\rangle,\left\langle e_{2},e_{2}^{\prime}\right\rangle\right\rangle}}}
.

By definition of
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\mathtt{gensend}\left(\cdot\right)}}}
:

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\mathtt{gensend}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left(\vdash\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left\langle\left\langle e_{1},e_{1}^{\prime}\right\rangle,\left\langle e_{2},e_{2}^{\prime}\right\rangle\right\rangle:\tau\times\tau^{\prime}}}}\right)}}}\right\downarrow}}\right)}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\mathtt{gensend}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left(\vdash\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left\langle\left\langle e_{1},e_{1}^{\prime}\right\rangle\right\rangle,\left\langle e_{2},e_{2}^{\prime}\right\rangle.1:\tau}}}\right)}}}\right\downarrow}}\right)}}};{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\mathtt{gensend}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0,0}\pgfsys@color@gray@stroke{0}\pgfsys@color@gray@fill{0}{\left.\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left(\vdash\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{\left\langle\left\langle e_{1},e_{1}^{\prime}\right\rangle\right\rangle,\left\langle e_{2},e_{2}^{\prime}\right\rangle.2:\tau^{\prime}}}}\right)}}}\right\downarrow}}\right)}}}}}}

By assumption we have HP1

Appendix 0.E
Composition results

In this appendix we deduce some insights from the composition of trace-relations and property mappings.
We describe the correctness theorem obtained for a multistep compiler, where each step
is proved correct for a possibly different trace relation.
We obtain the expected result: it is indeed possible to compose individual
compiler correctness proofs for each step, to prove the correctness of the whole
chain.

Assume
\downarrow^{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,0.5,1}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0.50}{0}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0.50}{0}{0}{s}}}}_{\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,1,0.5}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,1,0.5}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0}{0.50}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0}{0.50}{0}{i}}}}
is a compilation chain from a source to an intermediate
language and
\downarrow^{\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,1,0.5}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,1,0.5}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0}{0.50}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0}{0.50}{0}{i}}}}_{\mathbf{{\color[rgb]{1,0.23,0.13}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{1,0.23,0.13}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{0}{0.77}{0.87}{0}{t}}}}
from the intermediate to a target language.

language we use a
\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,1,0.5}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,1,0.5}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0}{0.50}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0}{0.50}{0}{verbatim}}}
,
\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,1,0.5}\definecolor[named]{pgfstrokecolor}{rgb}{0,1,0.5}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@stroke{1}{0}{0.50}{0}\pgfsys@color@cmyk@fill{1}{0}{0.50}{0}{emerald}}}
